SELECT [m].[Id], [m].[AgeRating], [m].[Country], [m].[Description], [m].[Duration], [m].[GenreId], [m].[Language], [m].[Poster], [m].[ProducerId], [m].[Rating], [m].[Title], [m].[VideoPath], [m].[Year], [p].[Id], [p].[Bio], [p].[Image], [p].[Name], [g].[Id], [g].[Description], [g].[Icon], [g].[Name], [g].[NameAr], [m0].[Id], [m0].[Bitrate], [m0].[Codec], [m0].[CreatedAt], [m0].[FileSize], [m0].[IsAvailable], [m0].[MovieId], [m0].[QualityType], [m0].[VideoUrl], [m0].[ViewCount], [s].[ActorId], [s].[MovieId], [s].[Id], [s].[Bio], [s].[Image], [s].[Name]
FROM [Movies] AS [m]
INNER JOIN [Producers] AS [p] ON [m].[ProducerId] = [p].[Id]
INNER JOIN [Genres] AS [g] ON [m].[GenreId] = [g].[Id]
LEFT JOIN [MovieQualities] AS [m0] ON [m].[Id] = [m0].[MovieId]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [a].[ActorId], [a].[MovieId], [a0].[Id], [a0].[Bio], [a0].[Image], [a0].[Name]
    FROM [ActorMovies] AS [a]
    INNER JOIN [Actors] AS [a0] ON [a].[ActorId] = [a0].[Id]

FROM [Movies] AS [m]
INNER JOIN [Producers] AS [p] ON [m].[ProducerId] = [p].[Id]
INNER JOIN [Genres] AS [g] ON [m].[GenreId] = [g].[Id]
LEFT JOIN [MovieQualities] AS [m0] ON [m].[Id] = [m0].[MovieId]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [a].[ActorId], [a].[MovieId], [a0].[Id], [a0].[Bio], [a0].[Image], [a0].[Name]
    FROM [ActorMovies] AS [a]
    INNER JOIN [Actors] AS [a0] ON [a].[ActorId] = [a0].[Id]
) AS [s] ON [m].[Id] = [s].[MovieId]
ORDER BY [m].[Id], [p].[Id], [g].[Id], [m0].[Id], [s].[ActorId], [s].[MovieId]

SELECT [m].[Id], [m].[AgeRating], [m].[Country], [m].[Description], [m].[Duration], [m].[GenreId], [m].[Language], [m].[Poster], [m].[ProducerId], [m].[Rating], [m].[Title], [m].[VideoPath], [m].[Year], [p].[Id], [p].[Bio], [p].[Image], [p].[Name], [g].[Id], [g].[Description], [g].[Icon], [g].[Name], [g].[NameAr], [m0].[Id], [m0].[Bitrate], [m0].[Codec], [m0].[CreatedAt], [m0].[FileSize], [m0].[IsAvailable], [m0].[MovieId], [m0].[QualityType], [m0].[VideoUrl], [m0].[ViewCount], [s].[ActorId], [s].[MovieId], [s].[Id], [s].[Bio], [s].[Image], [s].[Name]
FROM [Movies] AS [m]
INNER JOIN [Producers] AS [p] ON [m].[ProducerId] = [p].[Id]
INNER JOIN [Genres] AS [g] ON [m].[GenreId] = [g].[Id]
LEFT JOIN [MovieQualities] AS [m0] ON [m].[Id] = [m0].[MovieId]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [a].[ActorId], [a].[MovieId], [a0].[Id], [a0].[Bio], [a0].[Image], [a0].[Name]
    FROM [ActorMovies] AS [a]
    INNER JOIN [Actors] AS [a0] ON [a].[ActorId] = [a0].[Id]
) AS [s] ON [m].[Id] = [s].[MovieId]
ORDER BY [m].[Id], [p].[Id], [g].[Id], [m0].[Id], [s].[ActorId], [s].[MovieId]

FROM [Movies] AS [m]
INNER JOIN [Producers] AS [p] ON [m].[ProducerId] = [p].[Id]
INNER JOIN [Genres] AS [g] ON [m].[GenreId] = [g].[Id]

    SELECT [a].[ActorId], [a].[MovieId], [a0].[Id], [a0].[Bio], [a0].[Image], [a0].[Name]
